Connect your procurement agent

Start with an operator-issued sandbox credential. The operator provisions the scenario and provides its base URL, customer scope and any buyer mandate. Use an isolated sandbox deployment with its own database, signing keys, credentials and policy; set GATEWAY_ENVIRONMENT=sandbox. Do not point scenario clients at live demo storage. Orders are fixed-price, gateway-simulated; no order is sent to Valio Aimo.

OpenAPI · MCP server card (draft) · A2A card · Tool schemas · llms.txt · JWKS · Pinned versions

Endpoints and authentication

REST base: {{BASE_URL}}/v1. MCP Streamable HTTP: {{BASE_URL}}/mcp (POST JSON-RPC; GET is the protocol stream, not a card). A2A 1.0 JSON-RPC: {{BASE_URL}}/a2a with A2A-Version: 1.0. Public discovery begins at AI catalog. Use TLS in production and configure GATEWAY_PUBLIC_URL to the public origin so MCP accepts its Host and Origin. Runtime tools/list and initialize are authoritative; the draft server card is advisory.

Private agent HTTP calls, including MCP initialize, need Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN. Human REST calls can instead use a session cookie from POST /v1/auth/login with X-CSRF-Token on mutations. X-Agent-Id grants no authority. An optional buyer-issued VC-JWT goes in Agent-Mandate on each request. GET /v1/me and MCP get_agent_context show only your principal, customers, standing constraints, effective limits, rolling budget and supported operations. Operator and buyer approver credentials are separate from agent credentials.

MCP client configurations

The console operator issues keys with POST /v1/connections and an Idempotency-Key, supplying agent_id, client and known customer_ids. Copy the vtg_live_ (or sandbox vtg_sbx_) key from the initial 201 response; exact replays return key: null. Tenant admins use /v1/tenant/connections with their own account. GET /v1/connections lists key prefixes and last use; DELETE /v1/connections/{connection_id} revokes the key for both REST and MCP. New agents require human approval; an existing agent keeps its mandate and autonomy. The client field records a tool preference and grants no extra authority. ChatGPT and other remote clients can use OAuth code/PKCE sign-in through /oauth/authorize. The existing operator-consented buyer /mcp flow is also supported on the same endpoints; it keeps its own consent requests and grants.

Claude Code, from any directory, using the issued remote credential:

claude mcp add --transport http valio-aimo "${GATEWAY_URL}/mcp" --header "Authorization: Bearer ${AGENT_TOKEN}"

Claude Desktop local stdio configuration (replace absolute paths and the operator-provisioned agent ID). Stdio is a trusted local process: it loads server credentials and policy from its environment; it does not use remote Bearer authentication. Run it on the gateway host only. Never copy operator or supplier secrets onto an outside buyer's device. Optional buyer VC: append --mandate /absolute/path/mandate.jwt.

{"mcpServers":{"valio-aimo":{"command":"/absolute/repo/backend/.venv/bin/python","args":["-m","app.mcp_server","--agent-id","issued-agent"],"cwd":"/absolute/repo/backend"}}}

Generic MCP CLI (install mcp[cli]==2.3.0 to include the optional Typer CLI dependency) (development inspector, gateway host):

cd /absolute/repo/backend
MCP_AGENT_ID=issued-agent .venv/bin/mcp dev app/mcp_entry.py

Raw stdio command: cd /absolute/repo/backend && .venv/bin/python -m app.mcp_server --agent-id issued-agent. Standalone Streamable HTTP on port 8001 is also available by adding --http --port 8001; the main API serves the discoverable multi-agent /mcp on its own port. Run one process per database: stop the gateway before using a standalone entry point on its database, or set DB_PATH to a separate isolated database.

Deterministic Python reference buyer

From the repository root, install pinned clients/python/requirements.txt in a separate venv, then export GATEWAY_URL and AGENT_TOKEN using clients/python/.env.example. Optional AGENT_MANDATE is the VC-JWT itself. Run python clients/python/reference_buyer.py --scenario full_stock or --scenario split_stock. No LLM is used. The client initializes MCP, lists tools, reads context, selects a stored plan, quotes, orders, follows next_action and verifies the Ed25519 receipt against public JWKS. full_stock requests 10 L; split_stock requests 400 L and requires a provisioned split plan plus a policy requiring human approval. The client sleeps on WAIT_FOR_APPROVAL and polls status; it cannot approve its own order. The operator approves in the console or authenticated REST inbox. Other scenario fixtures an operator may provision: missing_evidence, shifted_date, expired_quote, approval_time_stock_change and supplier_timeout. This kit does not provision or reset sandbox state.

Procurement rules

A need requires customer_id, intent, quantity {value, unit} and deliver_by. Units are L, KG or SALES_UNIT. Values are strict positive numbers up to 1,000,000. deliver_by is today through +60 days in Europe/Helsinki. Explicit quote quantities are whole sales units, including KG, in allowed_lot_size steps with a minimum step of one, and at least minimum_order_quantity. Minimum quantity and order multiple are independent. Do not treat a sales unit as one litre. Tool and field tables below carry the complete schemas.

Customer standing constraints are unioned with each request at lookup, quote, order and approval. Missing allergen evidence never proves absence. Hard constraints cannot be approved away. Preferences may produce substitutes requiring approval. Use a returned plan_ref, or explicit lines with need_ref from the stored lookup. Prices cannot be negotiated. Quotes expire at the earliest applicable price validity, future cut-off or 30 minutes. On REFRESH_QUOTE, create a new quote and inspect its fixed total.

Orders become CONFIRMED, PENDING_APPROVAL, PENDING_EXECUTION or REJECTED. PENDING_EXECUTION carries RETRY_SAME_COMMAND and a positive retry_after_seconds; repeat the original placement key to reconcile its durable supplier intent. Authority is rechecked before any new supplier submission. Pending orders return next_action=WAIT_FOR_APPROVAL and poll_after_seconds. A human reviews the evidence_revision. Changed evidence produces REVIEW_UPDATED_EVIDENCE and must be reviewed again; pending orders may expire. Follow next_action, never infer recovery from error prose. RETRY_SAME_COMMAND means wait retry_after_seconds and resend identical arguments and idempotency key, including after a lost response. LOOKUP_REQUIRED means refresh lookup; FIX_REQUEST means correct schema fields; CONTACT_ADMIN means replace credentials or obtain scope; STOP means halt. REST failures use RFC 9457 application/problem+json, MCP uses isError and identical structured content. X-Correlation-Id links requests.

Lookup, mutation and read token buckets are shared across REST, MCP and A2A per principal. Default capacities/refill per minute are 60/60, 120/120 and 600/600; operator policy may tighten them. Read get_agent_context for effective buckets. RATE_LIMITED (429) and upstream 502/503 retry guidance carry intervals. Supplier calls are paced at 5/s; 429 pauses uncached calls for Retry-After (default 30 seconds). Cached results can continue.

REST walkthrough

Execute in bash with GATEWAY_URL and AGENT_TOKEN exported, jq installed and DELIVER_BY set to a date within 60 days. The response files are temporary. This example needs the full_stock fixture. Agent credentials cannot approve; an approver uses POST /v1/approvals/{id}/approve with approver and the current evidence_revision.

set -eu
CONNECT_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/v1/me" -H "Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN" > "$CONNECT_DIR/me.json"
CUSTOMER_ID=$(jq -r '.customer_ids[0]' "$CONNECT_DIR/me.json")
jq -n --arg customer "$CUSTOMER_ID" --arg day "$DELIVER_BY" '{customer_id:$customer,intent:"lactose-free milk",search_terms:["milk"],required_claims:["FREE_FROM_LACTOSE"],exclude_allergens:["AE"],quantity:{value:10,unit:"L"},deliver_by:$day}' > "$CONNECT_DIR/need.json"
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/v1/offers" -H "Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @"$CONNECT_DIR/need.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/offers.json"
jq --arg customer "$CUSTOMER_ID" --arg day "$DELIVER_BY" '{customer_id:$customer,deliver_by:$day,plan_ref:{lookup_id:.lookup_id,plan_index:0}}' "$CONNECT_DIR/offers.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/quote-request.json"
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/v1/quotes" -H "Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -H "Idempotency-Key: connect-quote-$(basename "$CONNECT_DIR")" --data-binary @"$CONNECT_DIR/quote-request.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/quote.json"
jq '{quote_id:.id}' "$CONNECT_DIR/quote.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/order-request.json"
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/v1/orders" -H "Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -H "Idempotency-Key: connect-order-$(basename "$CONNECT_DIR")" --data-binary @"$CONNECT_DIR/order-request.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/order.json"
ORDER_ID=$(jq -r '.id' "$CONNECT_DIR/order.json")
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/v1/orders/$ORDER_ID" -H "Authorization: Bearer $AGENT_TOKEN" > "$CONNECT_DIR/order.json"
curl --fail-with-body -sS "$GATEWAY_URL/.well-known/jwks.json" > "$CONNECT_DIR/jwks.json"
python clients/python/verify_receipt.py "$CONNECT_DIR/order.json" --jwks "$CONNECT_DIR/jwks.json" --customer "$CUSTOMER_ID" --agent "$(jq -r '.principal.id' "$CONNECT_DIR/me.json")"

Verify evidence

Keep the order JSON and JWKS. The independent verifier above checks detached Ed25519 JWS, customer, agent and gateway-simulated commitment. Tampered totals or status fail verification. A verified receipt proves this gateway's recorded decision, not supplier fulfilment. Signed quotes can also be verified offline with backend/scripts/verify_quote.py; retain quote.signature and its SHA-256 digest in the receipt. The authenticated order timeline and UBL export are at /v1/orders/{id}/timeline and /v1/orders/{id}/ubl.

Connection failures

SymptomNext step
401, CONTACT_ADMIN; expired, revoked or unknown credentialAsk the issuer for a replacement sandbox credential, update Authorization, reconnect and read context. Do not retry a rejected key indefinitely. Static operator-issued keys remain valid until removed or rotated.
404, 405, HTML or JSON parsing errorCheck the base URL and transport. REST is /v1; MCP is /mcp; /mcp/server-card is metadata. Use POST for MCP JSON-RPC, never stdio frames over HTTP.
Unsupported protocol version / initialize failure / 400Read supportedProtocolVersions from the draft card and pinned /v1/meta; use the pinned mcp==2.3.0 client and initialize before tools/list. A2A requires version 1.0.
403, CONTACT_ADMIN, missing customer scope or mandateCompare get_agent_context with the requested customer, then ask the buyer/operator for the correct scope or VC. Changing X-Agent-Id cannot grant it.
Lost connection / stale MCP session / 404 sessionReconnect and initialize again, list tools and read context. Retry an ambiguous mutation with the same key and payload; never create a new order key because a response was lost. This HTTP endpoint is stateless; it requires no session-ID persistence.

Accounts and connections

Human accounts sign in with email and password at POST /v1/auth/login. HttpOnly session cookies require X-CSRF-Token on mutations. Tenant admins manage their team, agents, signed mandates and connections under /v1/tenant. Approvers sign off their tenant orders; viewers read evidence. Platform admins use /v1/operator across tenants. Human decisions return a summary and a single-use five-minute confirmation token before applying the confirmed retry.

GET /v1/connect/catalog?audience=buyer|tenant|operator lists MCP, REST, A2A, webhook and tool-pack types, clients and current availability. API-key connections issue vtg_live_ or vtg_sbx_ keys once. Instructions, rotate (up to 24 h overlap) and revoke live under /v1/tenant/connections/{id}. GET /v1/me shows the connection, tenant and allowed customers. Bearer keys and legacy OPERATOR_TOKEN remain supported. OAuth code/PKCE is available for remote MCP and A2A, with signed-in human consent at /oauth/authorize. Human MCP resources are /mcp/tenant and /mcp/operator; A2A resources are /a2a/tenant and /a2a/operator. Tenant/operator webhooks and tool packs use the same audience. MCP decisions require input_required confirmation; A2A and REST use a single-use five-minute confirmation_token. GET /v1/connect/tool-pack?format=openai|anthropic|gemini&audience=buyer|tenant|operator generates function declarations and a REST executor. Read the availability flags before offering a client flow.

Generated tool and field reference

Generated by backend/scripts/build_developer_docs.py from the same registered schemas as runtime tools/list. The divergence acceptance test checks this page and the complete JSON schemas.

ToolDescription
create_intentCreate a tenant buying intent. Repeating client_intent_id returns its current quote/order across REST, MCP and A2A; changed payload requires an amendment. Overlapping demand requires supersedes or approved additional demand. Quote using intent_id and intent_version, then place once. Use this tool when you need to create intent. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
get_intentGet an intent and its current quote/order, revision, remainder and structured next actions under your own mandate. Use this tool when you need to get intent. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
amend_intentAmend a buying intent at expected_version. Changes after placement require human approval and the normal order change/cutoff rules; the original commitment remains until approved. Use this tool when you need to amend intent. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
resume_intentContinue a same-tenant intent under your own current mandate. Rechecks categories and spending authority, preserves pending human approvals and returns next actions without another order. Use this tool when you need to resume intent. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
get_agent_contextRead your authenticated principal, customer scope, mandate summary, effective per-order and rolling 24-hour limits, remaining budget, environment, rate limits, standing constraints and supported operations. Profiled tenants also receive guardrail_profile with business profile, initial tenant guardrails, threshold, four-eyes, anomaly sensitivity and per-field preset sources. Advisory snapshot: authority is revalidated on each mutation. No other customer's authority is exposed. Use this tool when you need to get agent context. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
get_replenishment_suggestionsList purchase rhythms within your current customer grants, supplier scope and buyer-mandate categories, predicted within horizon days (default buyer preference, normally 14). Returns confirmed-order dates, quantities, total paid, supplier_id, predicted date, confidence with reason and a plain-language suggestion. Supplier agents cannot read memory; shown suggestions are audited and emit suggestion.shown. Never buys. Use this tool when you need to get replenishment suggestions. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
prepare_suggestionPrepare a replenishment suggestion as a normal fresh lookup with fulfilment plans under your mandate. Optional idempotency_key prevents duplicate preparations across REST/MCP. Creates no quote or order; quote the returned plan separately, then place under normal policy and approval. Hidden, dismissed or out-of-scope suggestions return SUGGESTION_NOT_FOUND. Buyer authority works without a policy agent row; policy only tightens its per-order and daily limits and autonomy. Levels: approval_required (default when omitted) and autonomous_within_limits; the stricter buyer/policy level wins. approval_required adds autonomy_level and routes otherwise acceptable orders to PENDING_APPROVAL. GET /v1/agents/{id}/track-record gives advisory readiness; only an operator or the buyer_admin may POST /v1/agents/{id}/autonomy with level and reason (optional Idempotency-Key binds the target and payload, rechecks current scope and prevents repeated changes). Quote/order bind and expose mandate metadata; human approval revalidates the original stored mandate. Without a buyer VC, policy-row behavior applies. find_offersFind up to 10 ranked live priced offers for a structured need. function {product_type, intended_use, preparation?, acceptable_units} requires category and fit evidence; the fit question includes intended use. substitute_for {product_id} returns an available preferred product as best, otherwise ranks alternatives by category, claims superset, pack size and comparable price. Alternatives carry substitution {for, differences, allergen_evidence} and are described as compliant with the recorded procurement constraints. Current customer substitution profiles are re-evaluated at placement and approval against signed quote-time brand, pack and comparable-price evidence; missing historical comparison evidence needs review. Category closeness ranks substitutes; function.product_type determines eligible categories. clinical_review=required forces a scoped buyer_approver; an operator alone cannot approve. Union current customer standing constraints with the request; effective_constraints and exclusion_counts name request/customer_policy sources and evidence reasons. Decisions screens the catalog pool when configured; screened_count counts actual submissions (including failed requests), zero when skipped. Then one price and stock batch checks suitable products up to shortlist.live_max (default 25). Rank orderable offers first, then scored before unscored, then preference matches before substitutes. Unknown-size offers retain SIZE_UNKNOWN and compare their known sales-unit prices separately. Pack conversion uses Decimal arithmetic and six-decimal sizes; CASE content includes its multiplier once. Every sales quantity is whole, including KG: allowedLotSize below one counts as one. Price factor uses purchase_total (whole packs), overbuy {amount, unit} states excess, score_breakdown exposes weighted factors; unit_price is per sales unit. Reliability is absolute and CO2 uses the global catalog range; missing values use neutral catalog medians. Plans use ALL ranked priced offers before the returned slice, with fill and cheapest-split searches sharing a 20,000-node budget and valid greedy fallbacks, without extra calls; previews include mandate and daily limits. Placement rechecks stock/policy. Explicit search_terms use AND; otherwise intent uses AND, broadening only empty results deterministically to meaningful OR words and hyphenated parts. RETRIEVAL_BROADENED names tried words; POOL_TRUNCATED discloses unscreened matches. Returns retrieval {mode, terms_used}, matched_count, screened_count, priced_count, lookup_id, evidence, provenance, fit, stock and single/split/partial plans. no_match_reason explains empty hard-filter results and suggests search_terms after empty broadening. Spec 45: quotes and orders include anomalies with status normal/anomaly/insufficient_history, expected_band, observed, history_window, band_source and fixes. QUANTITY_ANOMALY, PRICE_ANOMALY, VELOCITY_ANOMALY and NEW_CATEGORY require approval. Under four observations no warning is raised. Placement rechecks history; twice the velocity band pauses the agent (423 AGENT_PAUSED) and emits an alert. apply_anomaly_fix re-quotes corrected quantity, a cheaper ranked alternative or a split; the remainder requires approval. A pending original order is superseded, never approved by the fix. Buyer administrators use the fix action_url to pause/resume. NeedSpec.supplier_id defaults to valio; nordfood is a synthetic case-priced supplier. A stored need_ref binds the supplier through quote and order; GET /v1/suppliers advertises capabilities. Supplier restrictions in policy and buyer VC supplier_ids apply before lookup and on commitment. EUR-only policy refuses SEK with UNSUPPORTED_CURRENCY. minimum_order_quantity is a lower bound; allowed_lot_size is the independent order multiple. Orders expose execution.mode/status/supplier_ref/acknowledged_at. Valio is gateway-simulated. Nordfood becomes supplier-acknowledged only after its service accepts; PENDING_EXECUTION with next_action RETRY_SAME_COMMAND and positive retry_after_seconds means repeat place_order with exactly the same idempotency key. Durable dispatch intents commit before supplier contact; recovery first reconciles the supplier key and rechecks current authority before any new submit. Sandbox is a separate deployment with sandbox-only credentials and storage, fixture provenance, environment=sandbox signed artifacts and UBL labels. Sandbox reset expires outstanding promises with reason sandbox_reset; admin routes require the sandbox admin credential. Warnings: PARTIAL_STOCK means insufficient on-time sales units; DATE_SHIFT means delivery after the deadline; CUTOFF_PASSED means the order cut-off for the accompanying delivery date has passed, and requires approval; cache hits advance the fetched supplier time by monotonic cache age, preserving fetched-at provenance. SALES_BLOCK means not for sale; NOT_AVAILABLE means empty schedule lines and rejects by default; UNKNOWN_PRODUCT is a valid legacy policy code, no longer emitted. AVAILABILITY_UNCONFIRMED means a usable schedule amount >=999999: stock unknown, ignored by default. When on time, any quantity is orderable, available_amount=null, availability_known=false, with no local reservation. Later unknown stock leaves finite on-time stock known and reserved. The amount rule applies to every logistic model; delivery dates, sales blocks and units still apply. PRICE_ERROR means invalid/unavailable price; SIZE_UNKNOWN means no unit conversion; SIZE_MISMATCH means pack content evidence differs by more than 3% and requires review. A live price ratio resolves catalog/name evidence; without it, contradictory sizes are shown together, the plausible catalog package total is retained and human review is required. Either language can supply a price-confirmed exact name candidate within 3%. Nested name counts retain inner packages while consuming the outer multiplier once; full nested pcs/kpl counts require reconciliation with a package total. Slash portion counts do not multiply the total; consistent shared-unit net/drained names use net weight. Zero/negative sizes are no evidence; OUTSIDE_PREFERENCE marks substitutes and lists differences from soft brand, price, pack-size or label preferences; these need approval by default. NO_FIT_SCORE means unavailable Decisions probability; AVAILABILITY_ERROR means stock is unverified. Hard constraints and sale blocks cannot be overridden by fit scores. SIZE_UNCONFIRMED, UNIT_MISMATCH (zero usable amount), STOCK_MODEL_UNKNOWN and CUTOFF_UNKNOWN require review; Name sizes require a confirming live ratio within 3%, matching explicit catalog content within 3%, one count expression matching the sales multiplier without contradictory measures, or a single measure at multiplier 1. Otherwise size_per_sales_unit and units_needed are display estimates from the smallest plausible candidate and SIZE_UNCONFIRMED prevents autonomous placement. Spec 15 also requires SIZE_UNCONFIRMED for catalog/name sizes without a confirming live ratio. name_size_candidates lists every candidate with original source_text and local expression. Explicit package totals are never multiplied by portion counts; repeated complete pack expressions stay independent, and slash portion counts are retained (100 pieces / 25 per 625g pack = 2.5kg). NO_STOCK rejects zero-stock products with no delivery date. Missing/zero/negative prices or missing currency give PRICE_ERROR. VARIABLE_WEIGHT (final price follows delivered weight) and DEPOSIT (details supplied) are informational. All warnings fail closed to approval unless explicitly ignored by policy; protected evidence warnings cannot be ignored. Naive closingTime uses Europe/Helsinki. Missing reliability/CO2 is neutral, not proof. Accounts: the credential binds a tenant, connection and allowed customers. create_quoteRecheck live price and stock for selected lines or a stored fulfilment plan, then lock fixed net EUR prices until expires_at (at most 30 minutes). Supply exactly one of lines, plan_ref {lookup_id, plan_index} or basket_ref {basket_lookup_id}. A basket reference selects its stored deduplicated lines, preserves ingredient constraints and uncovered warnings, and records all-or-none local simulation atomicity on its order; plan_index is zero-based, need_ref defaults to the plan lookup. Explicit lines REQUIRE need_ref to a stored lookup, otherwise 422 NEED_REF_REQUIRED. Current customer standing constraints are unioned with the stored request at quotation, placement and approval. Partial-plan uncovered warnings persist into ordering. Registered agents may quote only for customers in their mandate (CUSTOMER_NOT_IN_MANDATE); unregistered agents receive AGENT_NOT_REGISTERED on lookup, quote and order. Quantities are strict numbers >0 and <=1,000,000 in whole sales units, including KG. allowedLotSize below one counts as one; fractional quantities return 422 INVALID_LOT_SIZE naming the step. Invalid price or missing currency prevents quoting. Customer and delivery date must match the stored need. Ed25519 detached JWS over the spec 10/13 RFC 8785 quote_payload with decimal-string quantities/money and provenance; protected quote_version=2 and quote_integrity authenticate immutable evidence separately from the promise payload. Earlier unversioned signatures remain usable until expiry; kid names a public key at /.well-known/jwks.json. Offline: scripts/verify_quote.py quote.json --jwks jwks.json --customer ID. price_commitment=gateway-locked. Cached command replays revalidate the bound mandate and customer scope. Buyer authority works without a policy agent row; policy only tightens its per-order and daily limits and autonomy. Levels: approval_required (default when omitted) and autonomous_within_limits; the stricter buyer/policy level wins. approval_required adds autonomy_level and routes otherwise acceptable orders to PENDING_APPROVAL. GET /v1/agents/{id}/track-record gives advisory readiness; only an operator or the buyer_admin may POST /v1/agents/{id}/autonomy with level and reason (optional Idempotency-Key binds the target and payload, rechecks current scope and prevents repeated changes). Quote/order bind and expose mandate metadata; human approval revalidates the original stored mandate. Without a buyer VC, policy-row behavior applies. Use this tool when you need to create quote. Next call place_order. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
apply_anomaly_fixApply a server-proposed anomaly fix by quote_id and fix_id. Rechecks ownership and mandate, uses fresh price/stock, and returns replacement quotes only. Correct quantity, choose a cheaper ranked alternative, or split usual quantity and remainder (the remainder always requires human approval). Supersedes the original promise and any pending order. Pause/resume requires the buyer administrator at the proposed action_url; this tool cannot approve or resume an agent. Use this tool when you need to apply anomaly fix. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
place_orderAccept an unexpired fixed-price quote. Ordering requires a mandate: unregistered agents receive AGENT_NOT_REGISTERED with no order or reservation created. Lookup and quote also require a mandate. Registered agents must stay in customer scope, below the lower of global and mandate per-order limits, and within a cumulative rolling 24-hour autonomous spend limit; cancelled and human-approved orders are excluded. Rechecks stock and policy; returns CONFIRMED, PENDING_APPROVAL or REJECTED with every rule hit. Retry with the same idempotency_key to get the saved first response, even after cancellation, after ownership and identity checks (PENDING_EXECUTION resumes ambiguous supplier execution per spec 25), even for legacy quotes or retired signing keys; an order-bound buyer mandate is always revalidated, including on headerless retries. EXPIRED_QUOTE requires a new quote. Human approval uses the tenant/operator REST, MCP or A2A inbox tools, optionally bound to evidence_revision. Changed evidence stays pending with 409 EVIDENCE_CHANGED; a human must review the refreshed revision and use a new approval idempotency key before approving again. No upstream order endpoint exists. Accounts: the credential binds a tenant, connection and allowed customers. GET /v1/connect/catalog lists client setup and available audiences; humans sign in at /v1/auth/login and manage their own tenant at /v1/tenant. Sign-up at /v1/auth/signup stores a business profile and sourced guardrail preset; get_agent_context and transaction context expose that tenant's snapshot. Hospital/school orders require claims and allergen exclusions (409 CONSTRAINTS_REQUIRED). Human approvals enforce tenant thresholds (403 APPROVAL_LIMIT) and independent reviewers (403 FOUR_EYES); four-eyes-off self-approvals are flagged and audited. Remote MCP supports OAuth 2.1 code/PKCE consent at /oauth/authorize; buyer agents use /mcp, tenant humans use /mcp/tenant and Valio staff use /mcp/operator, each an isolated audience. Tenant governance resolves platform, tenant and owned-agent policy with source/version/author at /v1/tenant/agents/{id}/policy/effective. Lookups, quotes and orders record policy_version_id. Paused buyers receive 423 AGENT_PAUSED (CONTACT_ADMIN). Tenant autonomy promotions require policy change requests; requesters cannot approve their own loosening. Buyer authority: stdio --mandate FILE or HTTP Agent-Mandate plus the agent credential. EdDSA VC-JWT issuer/signature/nbf/exp/revocation/sub/customer/category checks run on every step; MANDATE_INVALID names the failed check. Buyer authority works without a policy agent row; policy only tightens its per-order and daily limits and autonomy. Levels: approval_required (default when omitted) and autonomous_within_limits; the stricter buyer/policy level wins. approval_required adds autonomy_level and routes otherwise acceptable orders to PENDING_APPROVAL. GET /v1/agents/{id}/track-record gives advisory readiness; only an operator or the buyer_admin may POST /v1/agents/{id}/autonomy with level and reason (optional Idempotency-Key binds the target and payload, rechecks current scope and prevents repeated changes). Quote/order bind and expose mandate metadata; human approval revalidates the original stored mandate. Without a buyer VC, policy-row behavior applies. Use this tool when you need to place order. Next call get_order_status. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
get_order_statusRead this agent's current order state, order_revision, evidence_revision, approval_reason (one sentence from recorded rule hits), expires_at and fixed-price lines. Pending: next_action=WAIT_FOR_APPROVAL and poll_after_seconds; confirmed: null; rejected/cancelled: STOP (rejection retains the approver comment); expired: LOOKUP_REQUIRED. Revisions increase on status/evidence changes, never on polls or retries. This does not submit another order or call upstream APIs. Use this tool when you need to get order status. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Follow next_action on every domain error.

Pending orders return 409 NOT_DECIDED with next_action=WAIT_FOR_APPROVAL and data.poll_after_seconds. All terminal statuses retain their distinct outcome, including rejection comment; commitment is gateway-simulated. Other agents' orders return ORDER_NOT_FOUND. cancel_orderCancel only a CONFIRMED order owned by the authenticated HTTP principal or the stdio --agent-id, before every known line cutoff. Rechecks current customer scope, suspension and the original order-bound buyer mandate on fresh attempts and exact replays; a presented VC cannot replace revoked bound authority. Lines without a cutoff do not block. Releases local stock reservations, records the reason and actor, and leaves fixed prices unchanged. No upstream calls. ORDER_NOT_FOUND (404) hides another agent's order; INVALID_TRANSITION or CUTOFF_PASSED rejects ineligible orders. Use this tool when you need to cancel order. Next call get_order_status. change_autonomyREST capability POST /v1/agents/{agent_id}/autonomy. Operator or this agent's buyer_admin grants or removes autonomy. Requires a reason; audits AUTONOMY_CHANGED with a track-record snapshot. Optional Idempotency-Key binds this target and payload, rechecks current target scope, and replays the first result without changing authority again; changed target or payload returns 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT. Runtime policy publication follows the enclosing command commit. Readiness never grants authority automatically. A stricter signed buyer mandate still wins. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
list_approvalsRead pending approval status of your own orders; humans decide through their audience. Use this tool when you need to list approvals. Next call get_agent_context to review current authority before further actions. Recovery codes: VALIDATION_ERROR (correct fields), INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE or WRONG_AUDIENCE (reconnect with consent), ORDER_NOT_FOUND/LOOKUP_NOT_FOUND/QUOTE_NOT_FOUND (check your own reference), MANDATE_INVALID/AGENT_PAUSED (contact administrator), RATE_LIMITED/UPSTREAM_BUSY (retry after supplied interval), EVIDENCE_CHANGED (review refreshed evidence), EXPIRED_QUOTE (create_quote again), IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (restore original payload). Follow next_action on every domain error.
